+<h2>Formatting Tags</h2>
+<p><b>This is bold text.</b>The b tag makes text bold.</p>

+<p><i>This is italic text.</i>The i tag displays text in italics.</p>
+<p><em>This is emphasized text.</em>The em tag emphasizes text.</p>
+<p><u>This is underlined text.</u>The u tag underlines text.</p>
+<p><mark>This is marked text.</mark>The mark tag highlights text.</p>
+<p><small>This is small text.</small>The small tag displays smaller text.</p>
+<p><del>This is deleted text.</del>The del tag marks deleted text.</p>
+<p><ins>This is inserted text.</ins>The ins tag marks inserted text.</p>
+<p><sub>This is a subscript text.</sub>sub makes the text appear below the normal text line.</p>
+<p><sup>This is a Superscript text.</sup>sup makes text appear above the normal text line.</p>
